If flip i 0->1 and j 1->0 (to keep weight), how P/N change? For a pair (a,b) at distance t, contribution to P[t] if bits (1,0) lower->higher. Bits (0,1) contributes to N[t]. Alternative: Maintain matrix? We can compute delta for all t when flipping two bits as sum of four delta flips:
Flip single bit p from 0->1 (weight changes) affects P[t] for all t:
- pairs with p as lower: distance t to q=p+t. Old contribution to P[t]: h_p old * (1-h_q). If old h_p=0 -> contribution 0. New h_p=1 -> (1-h_q) = 1 if h_q=0. Delta = 1 if h_q=0 else 0.
- pairs with p as higher: lower q=p-t. Old contribution (1? Actually P[t]=h_q(1-h_p)) q lower, p higher? If p higher old 0 -> contribution h_q *1. New p=1 -> h_q(0)=0. Delta = -h_q.
So for p 0->1: P[t] += I[h_{p+t}=0] for t where in range; P[t] -= I[h_{p-t}=1] for t where in range.
For N[t] (#0 lower,1 higher): p as lower: old 0 lower contribution (1-h_p)*h_q = h_q. New 1 lower -> 0. Delta = -h_q. p as higher: old 0 higher contribution (1-h_q)*0=0; new 1 -> (1-h_q). Delta = 1 if h_q=0.
Thus N[t] symmetric: delta_N[t] = - h_{p+t} + (1-h_{p-t}). For 0->1.

For 1->0 flip (delta negative of 0->1 after flipping? Need caution if two flips interact). Easier sequential: update P,N for first flip, then for second with new array? Or compute net delta from both flips with original. Could implement arrays of length n-1. Use vectorized boolean slices.

For a single flip p:
old_val = h[p] (0 or 1). If old 0 new 1:
 t_down = 0..n-p-1 (q=p+t)
 P[t]+=1-h[q]
 N[t]-=h[q]
 t_up = 0..p-1 (q=p-t)
 P[t]-=h[q]  # because old higher=0 gives contribution 1, new 0? Check: old P contribution for q lower to p higher: h_q (1-0)=h_q; new h_q(1-1)=0 => -h_q.
 N[t]+=1-h[q] # old 0, new (1-h_q)*1 =1-h_q.
If old 1 new 0:
 as lower: P: old 1*(1-h_q), new 0 -> -(1-h_q); N: old (0)*h_q=0, new (1)*h_q = +h_q.
 as higher: P: old h_q*(1-1)=0, new h_q*(1-0)=+h_q; N: old h_q? N for lower q to p higher: (1-h_q)*1 =1-h_q, new (1-h_q)*0=0 => -(1-h_q).
So old1->0:
 P[t_down] -= (1-h[q]); N[t_down] += h[q]
 P[t_up] += h[q]; N[t_up] -= (1-h[q])

Let's design a program that:
1. Builds several candidate families:

Important exact evaluation: For arbitrary binary h, C = max(c_pos,c_neg) * dx. We can compute from a ±1 using T formula:
For a length n, total sum zero (weight n/2). Let A[t] = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-t} a_i a_{i+t}, t=0..n-1. Let B[t]=∑_{i=0}^{t-1}a_i - ∑_{i=n-t}^{n-1}a_i. Then directed counts P,N? We need exact max over np.correlate. Let's verify formula and relation to C to ensure no off-by-one/lag indexing.

Define a_i = 1 if h_i=1, -1 if 0. Weight zero: ∑ a_i=0.
For t≥1:
P_t = # i=0..n-1-t with h_i=1, h_{i+t}=0 = in a: a_i=1,a_{i+t}=-1.
N_t = # i with a_i=-1,a_{i+t}=1.
D_t = P+N = # diff.
A_t = ∑ a_i a_{i+t} = #same - #diff = (n-t) - 2D_t.
B_t? P-N =? earlier (a_i - a_{i+t})/2? For each diff, if a_i=1,a_{i+t}=-1 => +1; if -1,1 => -1. Sum (a_i - a_{i+t})/2 = (∑_{i=0}^{n-1-t}a_i - ∑_{i=t}^{n-1}a_i)/2. Since total sum 0, ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-t}a_i = -∑_{i=n-t}^{n-1}a_i. Thus B'_t = P-N = (prefix_{n-t} - suffix? Let's compute S=∑_{0}^{n-1-t} a_i - ∑_{t}^{n-1} a_i = ∑_0^{t-1}a_i - ∑_{n-t}^{n-1} a_i. This is prefix_t - suffix_t (where suffix last t). Let B=prefix_t - suffix_t. Then P-N = B/2.
Then P=(D + B/2)/2 = (D+B)/4? Wait D and B have parity. max(P,N)=(D+|B|/2)/2? Since P-N=B/2, max=(D + |B|/2)/2 = D/2 + |B|/4. Express A: D=(n-t-A)/2. max_count = (n-t-A)/4 + |B|/4 = (n-t-A+|B|)/4 = T/4. Good. C=max_count*dx = T * (2/n)/4 = T/(2n). For t=0: A0=n, B0=0, T0=0, C0=0 for binary. Matches h(1-h)=0. Good.

For palindrome, B_t=0 if total sum zero? B_t=prefix_t - suffix_t. Palindrome total zero gives prefix_t=suffix_t, B=0. Then C=t? C=(n-t-A)/(2n)=D/n.

Given a, A length n, B length n, T length n. For swap i (a_i) and j (a_j): We can compute deltaA_t for all t. Flipping a_i from s to -s and a_j from -s to s (assumes opposite; if not no weight change). General using formula for delta A_t = ∑ boundary? Since A_t=∑_k a_k a_{k+t}. Changing two bits:
For a single flip at p from old val v to -v (v=±1), delta A_t:
- for t where p+t < n: term a_p a_{p+t}: old v*a_{p+t}, new -v*a_{p+t} => Δ = -2 v a_{p+t}.
- for t where p-t >=0: term from lower index p-t to p: a_{p-t} a_p: old a_{p-t} v, new a_{p-t} (-v) => Δ = -2 a_{p-t} v.
So ΔA_t = -2 v [ (a_{p+t} if p+t<n else 0) + (a_{p-t} if p-t>=0 else 0) ] for t>0? For t=0, A0 = n always if all bits ±1? Flipping one changes weight no, but for two flips A0 remains n? Prefix condition t>p -> t=p+1..n-1: B[t] += δ.
Suffix condition p >= n-t -> t >= n-p: if n-p < n -> B[n-p:] -= δ. If p=0, n-p=n none. If overlap, addition and subtraction cancel as needed. B[0]=0 unchanged (p<t not if 0, suffix maybe if p>=n? no). Good. Need if slices include t=0? no.

After swap, compute max T = n - np.arange(n) - A + abs(B), C = T.max()/(2n). We don't even need maintain T separate; compute T from A,B each eval O(n). But for many candidates, computing T.max each time O(n). We already update A/B O(n). Good.

For local search, we can propose swaps and evaluate by doing flips and T.max. Need efficient candidate selection.

Gradient for arbitrary binary with T formula:
C = max_t T_t/(2n). For smooth energy, weight w_t ∝ exp((T_t - maxT)/τ). Derivative wrt a_i for T:
T_t = n-t - A_t + |B_t|.
∂T_t/∂a_i = -∂A_t/∂a_i + sign(B_t)∂B_t/∂a_i (for real variables). ∂A_t/∂a_i for continuous linear (not constrained ±1): - (a_{i+t}+a_{i-t})? Actually A_t = ∑_{k} a_k a_{k+t}; derivative wrt a_i: a_{i+t} if i+t<n (as lower term? A_t includes a_i a_{i+t} if i≤n-1-t) + a_{i-t} if i-t≥0. Yes positive a_neighbor sum. T derivative = -a_{i+t}-a_{i-t} + sign(B_t)*I_prefix/suffix. But for binary flip from v to -v, ΔT linear ≈ derivative * Δa_i where Δa_i=-2v, so gain = -2v * dT/dv? We only need relative score for swap: linear Δ for flipping i: Δ_i = ∂E/∂a_i * (-2v_i). For swap i (v=-1 to +1, Δa=+2) and j (v=+1 to -1, Δa=-2): ΔE = 2(s_i - s_j) where s_i = sign? Let's define score s_i = ∂E/∂a_i. Flip p from v to -v gives Δ ≈ -2 v s_p. For v=-1 (neg->pos) Δ=+2 s_i; for v=1 (pos->neg) Δ=-2 s_j. Swap neg i to pos, pos j to neg: Δ=2(s_i - s_j). So choose high s neg, low s pos.

Compute s vector via w_t. A part: sA_i = -∑_{t=1}^{n-1} w_t (a_{i+t}+a_{i-t}). This is Toeplitz convolution as earlier. B part: sB_i = ∑_t w_t sign(B_t) [1 if i<t else -1 if i≥n-t else 0]. Same as G_B. Use FFT for A part. If n 500-2048, okay.

def autocorr_lin(a):
  n=len(a); L=1; while L<2*n: L*=2
  f=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
  ac=np.fft.irfft(f*np.conj(f),L)
  return ac[:n]
This returns circular with zero padding: ac[t]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-t} a_i a_{i+t} for t? Let's verify with small a=[1,-1], n=2. L=4, f, ac[0]=2; ac[1]=∑ i=0 a_i a_{i+1}=-1; ac[2]=? okay. Should work. For [1,1,-1,-1], A1 =1-1-1=-? pairs: 1*1=1,1*-1=-1,-1*-1=1 total1; FFT should. Good.

Update A/B for flip:
def flip_a(a,p,A,B):
 v=a[p]
 if v==0? no.
 # A
 if n:
   if n-p > 1: A[1:n-p] += -2*v*a[p+1:]
   if p > 0: A[1:p+1] += -2*v*a[:p][::-1]
 # B
 delta = -2*v
 if p+1 < n: B[p+1:] += delta
 q = n-p
 if q < n: B[q:] -= delta
 a[p] = -v
 # A[0] remains n
Need verify A slice lengths:
Lower: t=1..n-p-1, number n-p-1. A[1:n-p] length n-p-1; a[p+1:] length n-p-1? p+1 to n-1 length n-p-1. Good.
Upper: t=1..p, number p. A[1:p+1] length p. a[:p][::-1] length p (q p-1 down0). Good.
If p=0, upper none. If n-p=1 (p=n-1), lower A[1:1] empty.
B: prefix t>p, so t=p+1..n-1: B[p+1:] += delta. suffix condition t >= n-p: if p=0, q=n none; if p>0, B[n-p:] -= delta. Check p=n-1: suffix condition t>=1? n-p=1, B[1:]-=delta. Flipping last bit affects suffix for all t>=1, not prefix? prefix t>p none. Formula ok.

A part via FFT:
kernel zeros length L. For t=1..n-1: kernel[t]=w[t], kernel[-t mod L]=w[t]. Actually for weights w_t, operator T a = ∑ w_t (a_{i+t}+a_{i-t}). We can compute circular conv of a with two-sided kernel. Kernel centered at 0: k[t]=w[t] for t=1..n-1, k[-t]=w[t] corresponds index L-t. Circular convolution (a * k)[i] = ∑_j a_j k[i-j mod L]. For i in 0..n-1:
- j=i+t => k[i-j]=k[-t]=w[t]; need k[L-t]=w[t]
- j=i-t => k[t]=w[t]. So set k[t]=w[t], k[L-t]=w[t].
Then conv = ifft(rfft(a,L)*rfft(k,L)).real. Result length L; first n are G_A (without sign). This should be linear due zero padding if L≥2n-1. Good.

def local_search_binary(h_init, n, deadline, alpha_base, max_pairs, pert_size, rng):
  h0 = binarize_exactly(h_init,n); a=2*h0-1; A=autocorr(a); B=prefix...; ccur=compute C from A,B; cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy(); best_A? no.
  it=0; stalls=0; alpha=alpha_base
  while time<deadline:
    # recompute if drift or every stale
    if it % 1000 ==0: recompute A,B; ccur=...
    # weights/G
    G = compute_scores(a,A,B,n,alpha)
    pos_idx = np.where(a>0.5)[0]; neg_idx=np.where(a<-0.5)[0]
    if len(pos)==0 or len(neg)==0: break
    K = min(150, len(pos), len(neg)) maybe
    neg_sel = top_neg(neg_idx,G,K); pos_sel = top_pos(pos_idx,G,K)
    diffs = G[neg_sel,None] - G[pos_sel,None]
    order = np.argsort(-diffs.ravel())
    max_tries = min(max_pairs, len(order))
    best_pair = None; best_c = ccur
    for idx in order[:max_tries]:
       i=int(neg_sel[idx//K]); j=int(pos_sel[idx%K])
       # quick prune? apply flip/undo and c
       flip a,i; flip a,j
       c = maxT(A,B,n)*inv2n
       if c < best_c:
          best_c=c; best_pair=(i,j)
          # Could continue to find better among all? yes
       # undo j,i
    if best_pair is not None:
       apply i,j; ccur=best_c; stalls=0
       if ccur <= cbest + 1e-16: update best? If ccur < cbest: cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy()
       alpha = max(50, alpha*0.9) maybe
    else:
       stalls +=1
       # every few stalls perturb current (or reset to best if too many)
       if stalls > 8:
          if rng.random()<0.3: # perturb best to current? we need generate new current from best plus random flips
          else perturb current
       else:
          perturb current small if rng
       # recompute ccur maybe
  return best_a, cbest

Pseudo for local_search:
def local_search_binary(h_init,n,deadline,alpha,active_max,max_pairs,pert_prob,seed):
  h = binarize_feasible(h_init,n); a=2*h-1
  n_minus = n - np.arange(n)
  inv2n = 0.5/n
  recompute A,B; ccur; cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy()
  it=0; stalls=0
  while time<deadline:
    ccur, G = compute_scores(active) # returns ccur too
    # if current c differs from stored due drift? use it.
    pos = np.where(a>0)[0]; neg=np.where(a<0)[0]
    K = min(120, len(pos), len(neg))
    if K<1: break
    neg_top = neg[np.argpartition(G[neg], -K) if K<len else np.arange(len(neg))] # negative top high
    neg_top = neg_top[np.argsort(G[neg_top])[::-1]]
    pos_top = pos[np.argpartition(G[pos], K) if K<len else np.arange(len(pos))]
    pos_top = pos_top[np.argsort(G[pos_top])]
    # candidate pairs top by max diff
    # limit pairs tries to max_pairs
    # Instead of building full diff matrix if K large, K=80 ->6400.
    diffs = G[neg_top, None] - G[pos_top, None].ravel()
    order = np.argsort(-diffs)
    tried=0; best_pair=None; best_c=ccur
    for idx in order:
       i = neg_top[idx//K]; j=pos_top[idx%K]
       flip i; flip j
       c = maxT_current(A,B,n_minus,inv2n)
       if c < best_c - 1e-15:
          best_c=c; best_pair=(i,j); # can break if c <= target? maybe continue few
          if c < 0.35: break
       flip j; flip i; tried++
       if tried>=max_pairs: break
    if best_pair:
       i,j=best_pair; flip i; flip j; ccur=best_c; stalls=0
       if ccur < cbest - 1e-15:
          cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy(); exact? maybe not every update
       # adjust alpha maybe slight
    else:
       stalls += 1
       # Perturb
       R = 3 + int(min(50, n//200)) maybe increases with stalls
       if stalls > 12 and rng.random()<0.5:
          # reset to best then larger perturbation
          a=best_a.copy(); recompute A,B; ccur=cbest
          R *= 2
       for r in range(R):
          i = neg[random], j=pos[random] after possible update? Need choose from current
          flip i; flip j; # no T max, just update
       ccur = maxT_current(...)
       # alpha increase
       alpha = min(5000, alpha*1.3)
  # end: maybe exact recompute and project
  return best_a, cbest

Need define `flip_bit(a,p,A,B,n)` top-level returning nothing (modifies). Since many calls, passing n maybe not needed if slices with len. We'll write:
def flip_bit(a, p, A, B):
    v = a[p]
    n = len(a)
    if n - p > 1:
        A[1:n-p] += -2.0*v*a[p+1:]
    if p > 0:
        A[1:p+1] += -2.0*v*a[:p][::-1]
    delta = -2.0*v
    if p + 1 < n:
        B[p+1:] += delta
    q = n - p
    if q < n:
        B[q:] -= delta
    a[p] = -v
Need be careful A slice `A[1:n-p]` when n-p maybe n? for p=0, A[1:n] length n-1; a[1:] length n-1. Good. For p=n-1, n-p=1 -> slice empty. `A[1:p+1]` for p=n-1 length n-1, a[:n-1][::-1] length n-1. Good.

def autocorr_a(a):
    n=len(a); L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fa), L)
    ac[n:]? not needed
    ac[:n]
    ac[0]=float(n)
    return ac

compute_scores:
def compute_scores(a,A,B,n,alpha,active_max,n_minus,inv2n):
    T = n_minus - A + np.abs(B)  # allocate
    cmax = T.max()*inv2n
    # top lags
    m = min(active_max, n-1)
    if m <=0: return cmax, np.zeros(n)
    top = np.argsort(T)[::-1][:m]  # alloc
    top = top[top>0]
    if len(top)==0: return cmax, zeros
    vals = T[top]
    w = np.exp((vals - vals.max())*alpha)
    s=w.sum(); if s>0 w/=s
    GA = np.zeros(n)
    for idx,t in enumerate(top):
       wt=w[idx]
       if wt==0 or t==0: continue
       nt=n-int(t)
       GA[:nt] -= wt*a[int(t):]
       GA[int(t):] -= wt*a[:nt]
    # Bpart dense
    sB = np.zeros(n)
    for idx,t in enumerate(top): sB[int(t)] = w[idx]*np.sign(B[int(t)])
    total=sB.sum()
    cum=np.cumsum(sB)
    GA[:n] += total - cum  # pre for i=0..n-1: sum_{t>i}
    # suff
    # GA[1:] += - (total - cum[n-2::-1])? Let's verify length.
    if n > 1:
       GA[1:] += -(total - cum[n-2::-1])
    return cmax, GA
Test suff: n=5. For i=1, r=4, need -sum_{t=4} s_t. cum[n-2::-1] for i=1 index? n-2=3, cum[3]=sum t0..3, total-cum[3]=s4, negative. Good. i=4, start array at cum[0], total-cum0=s1..s4, negative. For i=1 if n=5, GA[1:] uses sequence [s4 contribution?], correct. If s0=0.
Alternative direct loop over top for Bpart may be simpler and less error:
  for t,wt in top: sg=wt*np.sign(B[t]); if sg==0 continue; GA[:t] += sg; GA[n-t:] -= sg (if n-t<n)
This O(active*n) but active_max <=128, n=2048 ->262k per score, okay. Simpler and avoids cum bugs. Use direct loops.

        GA[n-t:] -= sg

Perturbation: Random swaps preserving weight:
def random_perturb(a,A,B,n, num_swaps, rng):
  for _ in range(num_swaps):
    pos=np.where(a>0)[0]; neg=np.where(a<0)[0]
    if len(pos)==0 or len(neg)==0: break
    i=neg[rng.randint(len(neg))]; j=pos[rng.randint(len(pos))]
    flip_bit(a,i,A,B); flip_bit(a,j,A,B)
  return c_from_AB(...)

Implement `cont_gradient(x,n,alpha,active_max,mu)`:
  Hh=autocorr_arbitrary(x,n)
  total=x.sum(); pref=cumsum
  P[0]=total-Hh[0]; P[1:]=pref[n-1:0:-1]-Hh[1:]
  N[1:]=total-pref[1:n]-Hh[1:]; N[0]=P[0]
  v = np.concatenate([P[0:1], P[1:], N[1:]]) length 2n-1
  cvals = v * invn2
  cmax=cvals.max()
  top = argsort(cvals)[::-1][:active_max]
  y=cvals[top]-cmax; w=exp(y); w/=sum
  invn2=2/n
  w0=0; wP=zeros(n); wN=zeros(n)
  for idx,idx in enumerate(top):
    wobj=w[idx]
    if idx==0: w0 += wobj
    elif idx < n: t=idx; wP[t] += wobj  # P[1:]: idx 1 corresponds t=1; idx n? P indices 1..n-1 -> idx 1..n-1
    else: t=idx - n; wN[t] += wobj # idx n..2n-2 corresponds t=0? Wait idx n corresponds v[n]=N[1]? Since v: index0 P0, indices1..n-1 P1..P_{n-1}, index n N1. So for idx>=n, t=idx-n+1? Let's calculate: v length: 0: P0; for t=1..n-1: index t = P[t]; then for t=1..n-1: index n+t-1 = N[t]. Thus idx=1..n-1 P t=idx; idx=n..2n-2 N t=idx-n+1.
  So:
    if idx==0: w0
    elif idx < n: t=idx; wP[t]
    else: t=idx - n + 1; wN[t]
  g=zeros(n)
  if w0: g += w0*(1 - 2*x)
  for t in np.nonzero(wP+wN)[0]:
    wp=wP[t]; wn=wN[t]
    if wp+wn !=0:
       # neighbor sum
       # g[:n-t] -= (wp+wn)*x[t:]
       # g[t:] -= (wp+wn)*x[:n-t]
       g[:n-t] -= (wp+wn)*x[t:]
       g[t:] -= (wp+wn)*x[:n-t]
       if wp: g[:n-t] += wp
       if wn: g[t:] += wn
  g *= invn2
  if mu>0: g += 2*mu*(total-n/2)
  return g

Continuous refine function:
def refine_continuous(h,n,deadline,alpha,mu,maxit):
  h = project_feasible(h,n).copy()
  bounds=[(0,1)]*n
  res=minimize(cont_obj,h0,jac=cont_grad,method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, args=(n,mu,alpha), options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-24,'gtol':1e-18,'maxfun':maxit*10,'maxls':50,'maxcor':50})
  hp=project_feasible(res.x,n)
  cp=exact_c5(hp) # maybe use fast max via cont_c? Use correlate final? expensive but few.
  return hp,cp

Gradient:
  w=e/s
  wP=np.zeros(n); wN=np.zeros(n); w0=0
  # v[0]
  w0 = w[0]
  # P1 length n-1 indices 1..n-1 -> t=1..n-1
  wP[1:] = w[1:n]
  # N1: v[n:2n-1] length n-1 -> t=1..n-1

  g=np.zeros(n)
  if w0 !=0: g += w0*(1-2*x)
  K = wP + wN  # t=1..n-1
  # convolve x with K two-sided via FFT
  L=1; while L<2*n-1: L*=2
  kern=np.zeros(L)
  pos=np.arange(1,n)
  kern[pos]=K[pos]
  kern[L-pos]=K[pos]
  conv = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x,L)*np.fft.rfft(kern,L), L)[:n]
  g -= conv  # neighbor contribution: -∑K_t neighbors

def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  for loop:
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if small break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h; tot=room.sum(); if tot<eps break; h += (diff/tot)*room
    else:
      tot=h.sum(); h += (diff/tot)*h
  return clip

def to_binary_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.asarray(h,float)
  b=(h>=0.5).astype(float)
  target=n//2
  diff=target-b.sum()
  if diff>0:
    zeros=np.where(b==0)[0]; order=np.argsort(-h[zeros]); need=int(diff) maybe float; for idx in order[:need]: b[idx]=1
  elif diff<0:
    ones=np.where(b==1)[0]; order=np.argsort(h[ones]); need=int(-diff); for idx in order[:need]: b[idx]=0
  return b

def resample_h(h_old,m,n):
  if n==len(h_old): return h_old.copy()
  # Use nearest for step functions or linear? Linear introduces fractions but okay. Use linear interpolation on grid positions centers.
  x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False)+0.5/m? Actually sample centers. Use np.interp with left/right.
  x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
  return np.interp(x_new, np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False), h_old, left=h_old[0], right=h_old[-1])

Let's detail `project_sum` bisection without nested function:
```
def project_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = n * 0.5
    # If h has nan? no
    lo = -2.0
    hi = 2.0
    # Ensure sum at lo <= target? clip with lo: if all h+lo<0 sum maybe 0. lo = -max(h.max(),0)-2? hi = 1 - min(h.min(),1)+2?
    maxh = np.max(h); minh=np.min(h)
    lo = -maxh - 2.0
    hi = 1.0 - minh + 2.0
    mid = 0.5
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        vals = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = vals.sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return np.clip(h+mid,0.0,1.0)
```
This shifts all h by same amount, not the proportional room method. Is it correct projection onto hyperplane intersection box with L2? Yes adding a scalar to clip is Euclidean projection onto sum constraint and box. Good. But if target between, works.
If h already in box and sum target, mid near0.

`resample_h`: If h_in length m, n target. Use linear interpolation at sample centers:
```
def resample_h(h, n):
    m=len(h)
    if m==n: return h.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m,endpoint=False)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new,x_old,h, left=h[0], right=h[-1])
```
Maybe endpoint=False okay.

`make_random_run_h`:
```
def make_random_run_h(n, mean_run, rng):
    if mean_run <=1: mean_run=2
    p=1.0/mean_run
    a=np.empty(n); sign=1.0 if rng.rand()<0.5 else -1.0; i=0
    while i<n:
       run=int(rng.geometric(p))
       if run<1: run=1
       if i+run>n: run=n-i
       a[i:i+run]=sign; sign=-sign; i+=run
    h=(a+1)*0.5
    target=n//2
    ones=np.where(a>0)[0]
    diff=target-len(ones)
    if diff>0:
       zeros=np.where(a<0)[0]
       if diff>len(zeros): need=len(zeros)
       else: need=diff
       if need>0: chosen=rng.choice(zeros, size=need, replace=False); a[chosen]=1
    elif diff<0:
       ones=np.where(a>0)[0]
       need=min(-diff, len(ones)); if need>0: chosen=rng.choice(ones,size=need,replace=False); a[chosen]=-1
    return (a+1)*0.5
```
Ensure if diff negative after? okay.

Continuous objective/gradient: Use `autocorr_arbitrary` each call. Need next_pow2 many times; can cache? Top-level no global? Could use dictionary? Not allowed? We can define helper but okay. For speed, in cont_obj/grad compute L each call. Fine.
`autocorr_arbitrary`: For x length n. If n==0? no.
```
def autocorr_arbitrary(x):
    n=len(x)
    if n==1: return np.array([x[0]*x[0]])
    L=1
    while L<2*n-1: L <<=1
    fx=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    ac=np.fft.irfft(fx*np.conj(fx), L)
    return ac[:n]
```
`fast_c5`: use autocorr.
```
def fast_c5(h):
  n=len(h)
  Hh=autocorr_arbitrary(h)
  total=h.sum(); pref=np.cumsum(h)
  cmax = total - Hh[0]
  if n>1:
     p1=pref[n-1:0:-1] - Hh[1:]
     n1=total - pref[1:n] - Hh[1:]
     m=np.max(p1); if m>cmax: cmax=m; m2=np.max(n1); ...
  return cmax * (2.0/n)
```
But Hh[0] = sum squares, yes. For binary h sum target, p1/n1 maybe. Need if n=1 constraints impossible? skip.

Binary init_AB uses autocorr for ±1 but can use same `autocorr_arbitrary` and round. For speed, use same function.
```
def init_AB(a):
    A=autocorr_arbitrary(a)
    A[0]=float(len(a))
    A=np.rint(A)
    pref=np.cumsum(a)
    n=len(a); B=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1: B[1:] = pref[:-1] + pref[n-2::-1]
    return A,B
```

B update formula: Flip p, delta = -2v (new-old). B_t = prefix_t - suffix_t. If p<t, prefix gets +δ. If p in suffix (p >= n-t), suffix gets +δ, so B -= δ. Condition p >= n-t => t >= n-p. Our update B[p+1:] += δ; B[n-p:] -= δ. If p=0,n=4: flipping first bit. For t=1..3 prefix includes p? p<t yes => +δ. Suffix condition p>=n-t? 0>=4-t false. So B[1:]+=δ. Correct. If p=3: prefix p<t none; suffix condition 3>=4-t -> t>=1: B[1:]-=δ. Formula n-p=1 => B[1:]-=δ. Good.

`flip_bit` A update with t=1.. : For p=0,a update lower A[1:n] += -2v*a[1:]. Correct A_t for t includes a0*a_t, old to new Δ=-2v*a_t. For p=3 upper A[1:4] += -2v*a[:3][::-1]. Good.

Now binary `compute_scores_binary`:
```
def compute_scores_binary(a,A,B,n,alpha,active_max,n_minus,inv2n):
    T = n_minus - A + np.abs(B)
    cmax = T.max()*inv2n
    if n <= 1: return cmax,np.zeros(n)
    m = min(active_max, n-1)
    order = np.argsort(T)[::-1][:m]
    vals=T[order]; w=np.exp((vals-vals[0])*alpha); w/=w.sum(); 
    GA=np.zeros(n)
    for idx in range(m):
      t=int(order[idx]); wt=float(w[idx])
      if t==0 or wt<=0: continue
      nt=n-t
      # A part: -wt*(neighbor)
      GA[:nt] -= wt*a[t:]
      GA[t:] -= wt*a[:nt]
      # B part: sign(B[t])*wt prefix/suffix
      if B[t] >0: sg=wt
      elif B[t]<0: sg=-wt
      else: sg=0
      if sg!=0:
         GA[:t] += sg
         if n-t < n: GA[n-t:] -= sg
    return cmax, GA
```
Double-check Bpart direct: For each t, derivative of |B_t| wrt a_p = sign(B_t)*( p<t ? 1 : -1 if p in suffix ). So for all i<t add sg; for i>=n-t subtract sg..Slice GA[:t] corresponds i=0..t-1. GA[n-t:] i=n-t..n-1. Correct.
But A part for P? T_t = n-t - A_t + |B_t|, derivative of A_t is neighbor sum, so -wt*neighbors. We used GA[:nt] -= wt*a[t:], GA[t:] -= wt*a[:nt]. This adds contribution for i lower (i<n-t) to a_{i+t}, and i upper to a_{i-t}. Good.

Candidate selection notes: `np.argsort(T)` descending on counts. T[0]=0; not active. If T has negative? Should not: n-t-A+|B| = 4 max_count ≥0. For binary A can be n-t (all same) and B? T=|B|? But total zero prevents all same; D nonnegative; T=2D+? nonnegative. Good.

Local search binary objective with `c_from_AB`:
```
def c_from_AB(A,B,n_minus,inv2n):
    return float((n_minus - A + np.abs(B)).max()*inv2n)
```
Need n_minus passed. In local search, after each accepted we use `c_new = best_c`. Candidate eval compute and if best, store. But after accepting, A/B already flipped; use `ccur=best_c`. At end recompute.

But `local_search_binary` currently computes c from `compute_scores_binary` each loop; that's cmax. It also does candidate eval repeatedly; if A/B drift, recompute.

Local search detailed:
```
def local_search_binary(h_start, n, deadline, alpha=200.0, active_max=80, max_pairs=80, rng_seed=0):
    h0 = to_binary_feasible(np.asarray(h_start,float), n)
    a = 2.0*h0 - 1.0
    n_minus = n - np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    inv2n = 1.0/(2.0*n)
    A,B=init_AB(a)
    ccur = (n_minus - A + np.abs(B)).max()*inv2n
    cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy()
    n_args...
    stalls=0; it=0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # recompute periodically
       if it % 500 ==0:
          A,B=init_AB(a); ccur=c_from_AB...
       else:
          ccur,_=compute_scores... # But compute_scores computes T and ccur. We can combine: call returns ccur and G. It does T alloc. Good.
       ccur, G = compute_scores_binary(...)
       pos = np.where(a>0.5)[0]; neg=np.where(a<-0.5)[0]
       if len(pos)==0 or len(neg)==0: break
       K=min(max_pairs, active_max? maybe 80, len(pos),len(neg))
       if K<=0: break
       neg_top=... top K by G high
       pos_top=... low
       # candidate pairs sorted by diff
       diff_arr = (G[neg_top,None] - G[pos_top,None]).ravel()
       # To avoid trying same (no issue)
       order = np.argsort(-diff_arr)
       best_pair=None; best_c=ccur
       for pos_order in order[:max_pairs]:
          i = neg_top[pos_order // K]; j=pos_top[pos_order % K]
          flip_bit(a,int(i),A,B); flip_bit(a,int(j),A,B)
          c = c_from_AB(A,B,n_minus,inv2n)
          if c < best_c - 1e-15:
             best_c=c; best_pair=(int(i),int(j))
             if c < 0.36: # threshold break
                break
          # undo
          flip_bit(a,int(j),A,B); flip_bit(a,int(i),A,B)
       if best_pair is not None:
          i,j=best_pair
          flip_bit(a,i,A,B); flip_bit(a,j,A,B)
          ccur=best_c; stalls=0; it+=1
          if ccur < cbest - 1e-15:
             cbest=ccur; best_a=a.copy()
          # alpha small
          alpha = max(50.0, alpha*0.95)
       else:
          stalls +=1; it+=1
          alpha = min(3000.0, alpha*1.25)
          # perturb
          if stalls > 15 and rng.random() < 0.5:
              a = best_a.copy(); A,B=init_AB(a); ccur=cbest
          # random swaps count
          num = 3 + min(80, stalls*2)
          for _ in range(num):
             # choose
             pos=np.where(a>0.5)[0]; neg=np.where(a<-0.5)[0]
             if len(pos)==0 or len(neg)==0: break
             i=neg[rng.randint(len(neg))]; j=pos[rng.randint(len(pos))]
             flip_bit(a,i,A,B); flip_bit(a,j,A,B)
          ccur=c_from_AB(A,B,n_minus,inv2n)
    # final recompute
    A,B=init_AB(a); ccur=...
    if ccur<cbest: update best
    hbest=(best_a+1)*0.5
    # if best_a from earlier but current maybe better? use recompute
    return hbest, cbest
```
Candidate sorting `order[:max_pairs]`: If K=80 and max_pairs=80, order length 6400. Good. Could K too large; set K = min(80, max_pairs). active_max maybe 120 for score, but K=60.
Need rng: instantiate inside local search `rng=np.random.RandomState(rng_seed+it)`? Use one.

Potential bug: When we break after finding c<0.36, the current A/B are still for last evaluated swapped state? In loop, after eval we always undo. If break before undo? Code should undo before break. Structure:
```
flip i; flip j
c=...
if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_pair=...; found_good=c<0.36
flip j; flip i
if found_good: break
```
Do that.

At end, best_a might be from before but best_c from c_from_AB? Ensure best_h corresponds to cbest best_a. If current ccur improves cbest, update best_a. If later current worsens, best_a unchanged. Good.

Continuous refine after binary may use h from local search. But local search returns h binary. It may have c via AB; final fast_c5 should match. To ensure best_c accurate, we can recompute real objective after local search using `fast_c5(hbest)`. But AB formula exact for binary, still do.

`refine_continuous` may improve but also bound C. After L-BFGS, project and recompute fast_c5. Use `minimize` with `options` and `bounds`. Need `ftol`, `gtol` stringent.
```
def refine_continuous(h,n,mu,alpha,maxit,deadline=None):
    h0=project_sum(np.asarray(h,float),n)
    assert len(h0)==n
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for _ in range(n] # no lambda -> loop
    try:
       res = minimize(cont_obj, h0, jac=cont_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, args=(n,mu,alpha), options={'maxiter':maxit,'ftol':1e-24,'gtol':1e-18,'maxfun':maxit*10,'maxls':50,'maxcor':50})
       hp = project_sum(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
       hp = h0
    cp = fast_c5(hp)
    if cp > fast_c5(h0)+1e-12: hp=h0; cp=fast_c5(h0) # maybe not?
    return hp, cp
```
But if res.x infeasible due projection, hp maybe worse than h0; choose best of raw projected and h0. Also if mu caused sum off, projection may significantly worsen; choose.
Should we also test raw projected without penalty? okay.

`cont_obj` and `cont_grad` top-level:
Need avoid duplicate `fast_c5`? For objective use logsumexp to be smooth. But final objective can be max*2/n. `cont_obj` returns `m + log(s) + penalty`. This is greater than max; L-BFGS minimizes smooth surrogate. It might produce a solution with true max slightly higher/lower; final choose best true. Good.
Alpha: For n=1024, counts v ~ maybe 100; C ~0.38. Use alpha C units. In cont, c = v*invn2. alpha e.g. 500=> exp((c-max)*500). Differences 1e-4 => exp(-0.05). Good. Use alpha 1000/3000. For smaller n, C differences same; fine.
Potential underflow: `e=np.exp(c-m)`, c-m≤0, if alpha 5000 and diff 0.01 => exp(-50) underflows okay; sum at least 1.
Stable logsumexp: `s=e.sum(); if s==0: s=1`.

`cont_grad` exact for same LSE. Need weight arrays w from normalized exp. For all n lags (not active) to be exact. The gradient loop with FFT uses full weights; linear prefix via cumulative. Let's code carefully.
```
def cont_obj(x,n,mu,alpha):
    invn2=2.0/n
    Hh=autocorr_arbitrary(x)
    total=x.sum()
    c0=total-Hh[0]
    cmax=c0*invn2
    if n>1:
       pref=np.cumsum(x)
       P1=(pref[n-1:0:-1]-Hh[1:])*invn2

       m1=P1.max(); if m1>cmax: cmax=m1
       m2=N1.max(); if m2>cmax: cmax=m2
    # But need logsumexp, not just max. We'll compute vector for exact. To avoid allocate? For objective, allocate v.
```

`cont_grad` code:
```
def cont_grad(x,n,mu,alpha):
    invn2=2.0/n
    Hh=autocorr_arbitrary(x)
    total=x.sum()
    v=np.empty(2*n-1)
    c0=total-Hh[0]; v[0]=c0
    if n>1:
       pref=np.cumsum(x)
       v[1:n] = pref[n-1:0:-1] - Hh[1:]
       v[n:] = total - pref[1:n] - Hh[1:]
    c=v*invn2
    m=c.max(); e=np.exp(c-m); s=e.sum()
    if s==0: s=1.0
    w=e/s
    g=np.zeros(n)
    w0=w[0]
    if w0: g += w0*(1.0-2.0*x)
    if n>1:
       wP=np.zeros(n); wN=np.zeros(n)
       wP[1:] = w[1:n]
       wN[1:] = w[n:2*n-1]
       K=wP+wN
       L=1; while L<2*n-1: L<<=1
       kern=np.zeros(L)
       idx=np.arange(1,n)
       kern[idx]=K[idx]
       kern[L-idx]=K[idx]
       fx=np.fft.rfft(x,L); fk=np.fft.rfft(kern,L)
       conv=np.fft.irfft(fx*fk,L)[:n]
       g -= conv
       pref_wP=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(wP)))
       pref_wN=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(wN)))
       iarr=np.arange(n)
       g += pref_wP[n - iarr] - pref_wP[1]
       g += pref_wN[1:n+1] - pref_wN[1]
    g *= invn2
    if mu: g += 2.0*mu*(total - 0.5*n)
    return g
```
Check `pref_wN[1:n+1]`: For i=0 -> pref1-pref1=0; i=n-1 -> pref_n-pref_1 sum1..n-1. Good.
`pref_wP[n - iarr]`: for i=0 -> pref_n-pref1; i=n-1 -> pref1-pref1. Good.
Need wP[0]=wN[0]=0. Good.
Linear P condition uses t < n-i. If i=0, t< n => all t=1..n-1, yes. If i=n-1, t<1 none.
N condition uses i≥t. For i=0 none; i=n-1 all t=1..n-1. Good.
Potential issue: For t=n? none; arrays length n (0..n-1), wP[n]=0. Good.

The gradient of LSE objective `m+log(s)` wrt x: derivative = ∑ (e_i/s) ∂c_i/∂x = ∑ w_i ∂C_i. Since c_i=C_i? We set c = counts*invn2 (objective values); v counts. v[0]=count, c=C. So gradient of C_i = invn2 derivative count. We computed g counts derivative and multiply invn2. Good.

`minimize` with jac returns gradient; objective and gradient compute Hh twice. That's okay.

def enforce_sum(h,n):
  diff=n/2 - h.sum()
  if diff>0: idx=np.argmin(1-h); h[idx]=min(1,h[idx]+diff)
  else: idx=np.argmax(h); h[idx]=max(0,h[idx]+diff)
  return h

Add helper `tighten_sum(h,n)`:
```
def tighten_sum(h,n):
    h=h.copy(); target=0.5*n
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff>0:
       idx=int(np.argmin(1.0-h))
       add=min(1-h[idx], diff)
       h[idx]+=add
    elif diff<0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       add=max(h[idx]+diff,0.0?) Actually h[idx]+=diff (negative) clipped.
    return h
```

def next_power2(x):
    L=1
    while L < x:
        L <<=1
    return L
```

`flip_bit`: A and B arrays length n. If n=1, no slices. For n=1, local not used.
```
def flip_bit(a,p,A,B):
    v = a[p]
    n = len(a)
    if n - p > 1:
        A[1:n-p] += -2.0*v*a[p+1:]
    if p > 0:
        A[1:p+1] += -2.0*v*a[:p][::-1]
    delta = -2.0*v
    if p + 1 < n:
        B[p+1:] += delta
    q = n - p
    if q < n:
        B[q:] -= delta
    a[p] = -v
```
For n-p >1 condition length lower = n-p-1; A[1:n-p] length n-p-1. Good. For p=0,n=4, n-p=4>1, A[1:4] length3. OK.

def init_AB(a):
    A=autocorr_arbitrary(a)
    n=len(a)
    A[0]=float(n)
    A=np.rint(A)
    pref=np.cumsum(a)
    B=np.zeros(n)
    if n>1:
       B[1:] = pref[1:n] + pref[n-1:0:-1]
    return A,B
```

Continuous refine: Need ensure `minimize` is not called with `bounds` as list if n large; okay.
`refine_continuous` can take `alpha` and `mu`. We may run multiple times; maybe if time? L-BFGS no deadline. Use maxit.
```
def refine_continuous(h,n,mu,alpha,maxit):
  h0=project_sum(np.asarray(h,float),n)
  c0=fast_c5(h0)
  if maxit <=0: return h0,c0
  bounds=[]; for i in range(n): bounds.append((0.0,1.0))
  try:
     res=minimize(cont_obj,h0,jac=cont_grad,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,args=(n,mu,alpha),options={...})
     hp=project_sum(res.x,n)
     hp=tighten_sum(hp,n)
  except Exception:
     hp=h0
  cp=fast_c5(hp)

`tighten_sum`:
```
def tighten_sum(h,n):
    h=h.copy()
    target=0.5*n
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if diff > 0:
       idx=int(np.argmin(1.0-h))
       add = diff
       if add > 1.0 - h[idx]: add = 1.0 - h[idx]
       h[idx] += add
    elif diff < 0:
       idx=int(np.argmax(h))
       add = diff
       if add < -h[idx]: add = -h[idx]
       h[idx] += add
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
If diff beyond room (shouldn't), won't fully fix; `make_feasible` after project likely room enough. Could loop if not:

for _ in range(10): diff=... if abs<1e-12 break; apply; ...

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_power2(x):
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def autocorr_arbitrary(x):
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([x[0] * x[0]], dtype=float)
    L = next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    ac = np.fft.irfft(fx * np.conj(fx), L)
    return ac[:n].astype(float, copy=False)


def fast_c5(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    n = len(h)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    hh = autocorr_arbitrary(h)
    total = float(h.sum())
    cmax = total - hh[0]
    if n > 1:
        pref = np.cumsum(h)
        p1 = pref[n - 1:0:-1] - hh[1:]
        n1 = total - pref[1:n] - hh[1:]
        m1 = float(p1.max())
        if m1 > cmax:
            cmax = m1
        m2 = float(n1.max())
        if m2 > cmax:
            cmax = m2
    return float(cmax * (2.0 / n))


def project_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if n <= 0:
        return h
    maxh = float(np.max(h)) if n else 0.0
    minh = float(np.min(h)) if n else 0.0
    lo = -maxh - 2.0
    hi = 1.0 - minh + 2.0
    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    for _ in range(72):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        vals = np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        s = float(vals.sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return np.clip(h + mid, 0.0, 1.0)


def tighten_sum(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(12):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmin(1.0 - h))
            add = diff
            if add > 1.0 - h[idx]:
                add = 1.0 - h[idx]
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            add = diff
            if add < -h[idx]:
                add = -h[idx]
            if add >= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def make_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) != n:
        h = resample_h(h, n)
    if np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
        diff = 0.5 * n - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-11 * max(1, n):
            return tighten_sum(h, n)
    h = project_sum(h, n)
    return tighten_sum(h, n)


def to_binary_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if len(h) != n:
        h = resample_h(h, n)
    b = (h >= 0.5).astype(float)
    target = int(round(0.5 * n))
    diff = target - int(b.sum())
    if diff > 0:
        zeros = np.where(b == 0.0)[0]
        if len(zeros) and diff > 0:
            order = np.argsort(-h[zeros])
            need = min(diff, len(zeros))
            b[zeros[order[:need]]] = 1.0
    elif diff < 0:
        ones = np.where(b == 1.0)[0]
        if len(ones) and diff < 0:
            order = np.argsort(h[ones])
            need = min(-diff, len(ones))
            b[ones[order[:need]]] = 0.0
    return b


def resample_h(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    m = len(h)
    if m == n:
        return h.copy()
    if m <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 0.5)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(h[0]))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, h, left=float(h[0]), right=float(h[-1]))


def make_random_run_h(n, mean_run, rng):
    if mean_run <= 1.5:
        mean_run = 2.0
    p = 1.0 / float(mean_run)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    sign = 1.0 if rng.rand() < 0.5 else -1.0
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        run = int(rng.geometric(p))
        if run < 1:
            run = 1
        if i + run > n:
            run = n - i
        if run < 1:
            run = 1
        a[i:i + run] = sign
        sign = -sign
        i += run
    h = 0.5 * (a + 1.0)
    return to_binary_feasible(h, n)


def init_AB(a):
    n = len(a)
    A = autocorr_arbitrary(a)
    A[0] = float(n)
    A = np.rint(A)
    pref = np.cumsum(a)
    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n > 1:
        B[1:] = pref[1:n] + pref[n - 1:0:-1]
    return A, B


def c_from_AB(A, B, n_minus, inv2n):
    return float((n_minus - A + np.abs(B)).max() * inv2n)


def flip_bit(a, p, A, B):
    v = float(a[p])
    n = len(a)
    if n - p > 1:
        A[1:n - p] += -2.0 * v * a[p + 1:]
    if p > 0:
        A[1:p + 1] += -2.0 * v * a[:p][::-1]
    delta = -2.0 * v
    if p + 1 < n:
        B[p + 1:] += delta
    q = n - p
    if q < n:
        B[q:] -= delta
    a[p] = -v


def compute_scores_binary(a, A, B, n, alpha, active_max, n_minus, inv2n):
    T = n_minus - A + np.abs(B)
    cmax = float(T.max() * inv2n)
    if n <= 1:
        return cmax, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    m = int(min(active_max, n - 1))
    if m <= 0:
        return cmax, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    order = np.argsort(T)[::-1][:m]
    if len(order) == 0:
        return cmax, np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    vals = T[order]
    w = np.exp(alpha * (vals - vals[0]))
    s = w.sum()
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(w)
        w[0] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for idx in range(len(order)):
        t = int(order[idx])
        wt = float(w[idx])
        if t == 0 or wt <= 0.0:
            continue
        nt = n - t
        G[:nt] -= wt * a[t:]
        G[t:] -= wt * a[:nt]
        if B[t] > 0.0:
            sg = wt
        elif B[t] < 0.0:
            sg = -wt
        else:
            sg = 0.0
        if sg != 0.0:
            G[:t] += sg
            if n - t < n:
                G[n - t:] -= sg
    return cmax, G


def local_search_binary(h_start, n, deadline, alpha, active_max, max_pairs, rng_seed):
    h0 = to_binary_feasible(np.asarray(h_start, dtype=float), n)
    a = 2.0 * h0 - 1.0
    n = len(a)
    n_minus = n - np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    inv2n = 1.0 / (2.0 * n)
    A, B = init_AB(a)
    ccur = c_from_AB(A, B, n_minus, inv2n)
    cbest = ccur
    best_a = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(rng_seed)
    stalls = 0
    it = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if it % 400 == 0:
            A, B = init_AB(a)
        ccur, G = compute_scores_binary(a, A, B, n, alpha, active_max, n_minus, inv2n)
        pos = np.where(a > 0.5)[0]
        neg = np.where(a < -0.5)[0]
        if len(pos) == 0 or len(neg) == 0:
            break

        K = int(min(max_pairs, active_max, len(pos), len(neg)))
        if K <= 0:
            break

        nv = G[neg]
        neg_top = neg[np.argsort(nv)[::-1][:K]]
        pv = G[pos]
        pos_top = pos[np.argsort(pv)[:K]]

        diffs = (G[neg_top, None] - G[pos_top, None]).ravel()
        if len(diffs) == 0:
            break
        order = np.argsort(-diffs)

        best_pair = None
        best_c = ccur
        stop_early = False

        for pos_order in order[:int(max_pairs)]:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            ii = int(pos_order // K)
            jj = int(pos_order % K)
            i = int(neg_top[ii])
            j = int(pos_top[jj])

            flip_bit(a, i, A, B)
            flip_bit(a, j, A, B)
            c = c_from_AB(A, B, n_minus, inv2n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c
                best_pair = (i, j)
                stop_early = (c < 0.36)
            flip_bit(a, j, A, B)
            flip_bit(a, i, A, B)
            if stop_early:
                break

        it += 1
        if best_pair is not None:
            i, j = best_pair
            flip_bit(a, i, A, B)
            flip_bit(a, j, A, B)
            ccur = best_c
            stalls = 0
            if ccur < cbest - 1e-15:
                cbest = ccur
                best_a = a.copy()
            alpha = max(60.0, 0.9 * alpha)
            if cbest < 0.362 and rng.rand() < 0.2:
                break
        else:
            stalls += 1
            alpha = min(4000.0, 1.25 * alpha)
            if stalls > 18 and rng.rand() < 0.55:
                a = best_a.copy()
                A, B = init_AB(a)
                ccur = cbest

            num_swaps = 4 + min(90, 2 * stalls)
            for _ in range(num_swaps):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                p2 = np.where(a > 0.5)[0]
                q2 = np.where(a < -0.5)[0]
                if len(p2) == 0 or len(q2) == 0:
                    break
                i = int(q2[rng.randint(len(q2))])
                j = int(p2[rng.randint(len(p2))])
                flip_bit(a, i, A, B)
                flip_bit(a, j, A, B)
            A, B = init_AB(a)
            ccur = c_from_AB(A, B, n_minus, inv2n)

    A, B = init_AB(a)
    ccur = c_from_AB(A, B, n_minus, inv2n)
    if ccur < cbest:
        cbest = ccur
        best_a = a.copy()

    hbest = 0.5 * (best_a + 1.0)
    cbest = float(fast_c5(hbest))
    return hbest, cbest


def cont_obj(x, n, mu, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = len(x)
    hh = autocorr_arbitrary(x)
    total = float(x.sum())
    v = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c0 = total - hh[0]
    v[0] = c0
    if n > 1:
        pref = np.cumsum(x)
        v[1:n] = pref[n - 1:0:-1] - hh[1:]
        v[n:] = total - pref[1:n] - hh[1:]
    c = v * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(c.max())
    y = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    obj = m + (np.log(s) / alpha)
    if mu > 0.0:
        obj += mu * (total - 0.5 * n) ** 2
    return float(obj)


def cont_grad(x, n, mu, alpha):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = len(x)
    hh = autocorr_arbitrary(x)
    total = float(x.sum())
    v = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    c0 = total - hh[0]
    v[0] = c0
    if n > 1:
        pref = np.cumsum(x)
        v[1:n] = pref[n - 1:0:-1] - hh[1:]
        v[n:] = total - pref[1:n] - hh[1:]
    c = v * (2.0 / n)
    m = float(c.max())
    y = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        s = 1.0
    w = e / s

    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        g[0] = float(w[0]) * (1.0 - 2.0 * x[0]) * (2.0 / n)
    else:
        w0 = float(w[0])
        if w0 != 0.0:
            g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)

        wP = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        wN = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        wP[1:] = w[1:n]
        wN[1:] = w[n:2 * n - 1]

        K = wP + wN
        L = next_power2(2 * n - 1)
        kern = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        idx = np.arange(1, n)
        kern[idx] = K[idx]
        kern[L - idx] = K[idx]
        fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
        fk = np.fft.rfft(kern, L)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fx * fk, L)[:n]
        g -= conv

        pref_wP = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(wP)))
        pref_wN = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(wN)))
        iarr = np.arange(n)
        g += pref_wP[n - iarr] - pref_wP[1]
        g += pref_wN[1:n + 1] - pref_wN[1]
        g *= (2.0 / n)

    if mu > 0.0:
        g += 2.0 * mu * (total - 0.5 * n)
    return g


def refine_continuous(h, n, mu, alpha, maxit):
    h0 = make_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), n)
    c0 = fast_c5(h0)
    if maxit <= 0:
        return h0, c0

    bounds = []
    for _ in range(n):
        bounds.append((0.0, 1.0))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            cont_obj,
            h0,
            jac=cont_grad,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            args=(n, mu, alpha),
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxit),
                "ftol": 1e-24,
                "gtol": 1e-18,
                "maxfun": int(max(10 * maxit, 100)),
                "maxls": 50,
                "maxcor": 64,
            },
        )
        hp = make_feasible(res.x, n)
        cp = fast_c5(hp)
        if cp < c0 - 1e-15:
            return hp, cp
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h0, c0


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget_s) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        hp = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        n_parent = int(len(hp))
    except Exception:
        hp = make_random_run_h(500, 50.0, rng)
        n_parent = 500

    if n_parent < 2 or not np.all(np.isfinite(hp)):
        hp = make_random_run_h(500, 50.0, rng)
        n_parent = 500

    if n_parent % 2 == 1:
        hp = resample_h(hp, 500)
        n_parent = 500

    hp = make_feasible(hp, n_parent)
    best_h = hp
    best_c = fast_c5(hp)
    best_n = n_parent

    parent_h = hp.copy()

    for alpha in (500.0, 1500.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 60:
            break
        h_ref, c_ref = refine_continuous(parent_h, n_parent, mu=0.06, alpha=alpha, maxit=350)
        h_ref, c_ref = make_feasible(h_ref, n_parent), fast_c5(make_feasible(h_ref, n_parent))
        if c_ref < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_ref, c_ref, n_parent

    local_ns = []
    seen = set()
    for cand in (n_parent, 1024, 1536):
        if cand % 2 == 0 and cand <= 1600 and cand not in seen:
            local_ns.append(cand)
            seen.add(cand)
    if not local_ns:
        local_ns = [1024]

    budgets = {}
    for i, nloc in enumerate(local_ns):
        if nloc == n_parent:
            budgets[nloc] = 150.0
        elif nloc == 1024:
            budgets[nloc] = 360.0
        else:
            budgets[nloc] = 150.0

    for nloc in local_ns:
        if time.time() > deadline - 180:
            break

        h_start = parent_h if nloc == n_parent else resample_h(parent_h, nloc)
        h_start = make_feasible(h_start, nloc)

        h_pre, c_pre = refine_continuous(h_start, nloc, mu=0.05, alpha=700.0, maxit=250)
        if c_pre < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = make_feasible(h_pre, nloc), c_pre, nloc

        if nloc <= 600:
            active_max = 140
            max_pairs = 110
            alpha0 = 350.0
        elif nloc <= 1200:
            active_max = 100
            max_pairs = 75
            alpha0 = 600.0
        else:
            active_max = 75
            max_pairs = 45
            alpha0 = 900.0

        job_end = min(deadline - 150.0, time.time() + budgets[nloc])
        if job_end - time.time() < 30.0:
            continue

        h_bin = to_binary_feasible(h_pre, nloc)
        h_bs, c_bs = local_search_binary(
            h_bin, nloc, job_end, alpha=alpha0,
            active_max=active_max, max_pairs=max_pairs,
            rng_seed=int(rng.randint(0, 10**9))
        )
        h_bs = make_feasible(h_bs, nloc)
        c_bs = fast_c5(h_bs)
        if c_bs < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_bs, c_bs, nloc

        if time.time() < deadline - 120:
            h_ref, c_ref = refine_continuous(h_bs, nloc, mu=0.02, alpha=1800.0, maxit=550)
            h_ref = make_feasible(h_ref, nloc)
            c_ref = fast_c5(h_ref)
            if c_ref < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_ref, c_ref, nloc

    if 1024 not in local_ns and time.time() < deadline - 220:
        h_rand = make_random_run_h(1024, 70.0, rng)
        h_ref, c_ref = refine_continuous(h_rand, 1024, mu=0.05, alpha=800.0, maxit=250)
        if c_ref < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = make_feasible(h_ref, 1024), c_ref, 1024
        h_rand_bin = to_binary_feasible(h_ref, 1024)
        job_end = min(deadline - 140.0, time.time() + 110.0)
        if job_end - time.time() > 30:
            h_bs, c_bs = local_search_binary(
                h_rand_bin, 1024, job_end, alpha=700.0,
                active_max=100, max_pairs=70,
                rng_seed=int(rng.randint(0, 10**9))
            )
            h_bs = make_feasible(h_bs, 1024)
            c_bs = fast_c5(h_bs)
            if c_bs < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = h_bs, c_bs, 1024

    if time.time() < deadline - 140 and best_n % 2 == 0 and best_n <= 1600:
        if best_n <= 600:
            active_max = 140
            max_pairs = 120
            alpha0 = 500.0
        elif best_n <= 1200:
            active_max = 100
            max_pairs = 80
            alpha0 = 800.0
        else:
            active_max = 80
            max_pairs = 50
            alpha0 = 1100.0
        h_bin = to_binary_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        h_fin, c_fin = local_search_binary(
            h_bin, best_n, deadline - 60.0, alpha=alpha0,
            active_max=active_max, max_pairs=max_pairs,
            rng_seed=123456
        )
        h_fin = make_feasible(h_fin, best_n)
        c_fin = fast_c5(h_fin)
        if c_fin < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_fin, c_fin, best_n

    for alpha in (1200.0, 3000.0, 7000.0):
        if time.time() > deadline - 35:
            break
        h_ref, c_ref = refine_continuous(best_h, best_n, mu=0.01, alpha=alpha, maxit=800)
        h_ref = make_feasible(h_ref, best_n)
        c_ref = fast_c5(h_ref)
        if c_ref < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = h_ref, c_ref, best_n

    best_h = make_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = float(fast_c5(best_h))
    return np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), best_c, int(best_n)
```
